Kết quả chấm bài

Unique defects: 1) Missing hash evidence in chunk_17, 2) Placeholder PID in chunk_3, 3) Unresolved evidence reference in chunk_19, 4) Coarse timeline in chunk_14, 5) Misread privilege in chunk_12.
